<start_des>
The new diving heuristic function aims to prioritize rounding up variables that have a larger positive objective coefficient, while also considering the potential impact on feasibility and the fractional part of the solution. The rounding direction is determined first, and then the score is calculated accordingly, favoring variables that are more likely to improve the objective function while minimizing the risk of infeasibility.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = obj > 0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        roundup = False
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        roundup = True

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostup * (1 + nlocksup) - (rootsolval - candsol) ** 2
    else:
        score = -obj * objnorm * candsfrac - pscostdown * (1 + nlocksdown) - (rootsolval - candsol) ** 2

    if isBinary:
        score *= 2  # Prioritize binary variables

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_des>
The new heuristic scoring function, `myheurdiving`, aims to prioritize variables that have a higher potential impact on the objective function value while considering the feasibility of the rounding direction. The logic is as follows:

1. If the variable is binary, prefer rounding up if the objective coefficient is positive, and rounding down if it is negative. This follows the principle of maximizing or minimizing the objective function value.
2. For non-binary variables, consider both rounding directions and compute a score based on the objective coefficient, fractional part of the solution, and pseudo-cost values.
3. Penalize variables that have locks or are not allowed to round in a certain direction to encourage exploration of feasible solutions.
4. Favor variables with a larger objective coefficient and a larger fractional part to have a greater potential impact on the objective function value.
5. Incorporate the pseudo-cost values to guide the rounding direction that is likely to yield a better objective function value based on previous branching decisions.
6. Break ties using the Euclidean norm of the objective function vector, number of non-zeros, and root solution value.
</end_des>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    score_down = float('-inf')
    score_up = float('-inf')

    if isBinary:
        if obj > 0:
            score_up = obj
            roundup = True
        else:
            score_down = -obj
            roundup = False
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score_down = obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostdown
        if mayroundup:
            score_up = obj * candsfrac - pscostup

        score_down -= nlocksdown
        score_up -= nlocksup

        if score_down > score_up:
            roundup = False
        else:
            roundup = True

    if score_down == score_up:
        score = objnorm * (1 - abs(candsol - rootsolval)) * (nNonz + 1)
    else:
        score = max(score_down, score_up)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

This new score function aims to prioritize variables with larger objective coefficients and penalize variables that have already been locked or rounded in a particular direction. It also considers the fractional part of the solution value and the pseudo-costs associated with rounding up or down. The logic is to first determine the rounding direction that would yield a larger objective function value, considering the pseudo-costs. Then, the score is calculated based on the objective coefficient, fractional part, and penalties for locked variables.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    
    # Determine rounding direction based on objective value and pseudo-costs
    if obj > 0:
        roundup = (candsol + pscostup) >= (candsol - pscostdown)
    else:
        roundup = (candsol - pscostdown) >= (candsol + pscostup)
    
    # Calculate score
    score = abs(obj) / (objnorm + 1e-8)  # Normalize objective coefficient
    score += candsfrac if roundup else (1 - candsfrac)  # Prioritize larger fractional part
    score -= 0.1 * (nlocksdown if roundup else nlocksup)  # Penalize locked variables
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    if mayrounddown:
        score = (1 - candsfrac) * (obj / objnorm) * (1 + rootsolval) / (nlocksdown + 1)
        roundup = False
    elif mayroundup:
        score = candsfrac * (obj / objnorm) * (1 + rootsolval) / (nlocksup + 1) - (nNonz if isBinary else 0)
        roundup = True
    else:
        score = -1
        roundup = False
    return score, roundup

The scoring function aims to prioritize variables with a larger objective coefficient, encouraging rounding in the direction that increases the objective value. Additionally, it penalizes variables with a higher pseudo-cost, as these have proven difficult to optimize in previous iterations. <start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = obj >= 0
    if abs(candsfrac - 0.5) < 1e-6:
        roundup = rootsolval >= candsol
    
    # Calculate score
    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    score -= pscostdown if roundup else pscostup
    score += abs(candsfrac - 0.5) if roundup else 1 - abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    score += nNonz
    if isBinary:
        score += 1
    
    # Penalize if rounding is not possible
    if (roundup and not mayroundup) or (not roundup and not mayrounddown):
        score -= 10
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if obj > 0:
        roundup = True
        locks = nlocksup
        pscost = pscostup
    else:
        roundup = False
        locks = nlocksdown
        pscost = pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    fractional_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)  # Prioritize variables closer to 0.5
    objective_score = abs(obj / objnorm) if objnorm > 0 else abs(obj)  # Normalize objective coefficient
    lock_penalty = locks / max(1, nNonz)  # Penalize potential locking, scaled by non-zero entries
    pscost_penalty = pscost / max(1e-6, abs(obj))  # Penalize high pseudo-costs, scaled by objective coefficient
    exploration_penalty = 1 if (mayrounddown and mayroundup) else 0  # Penalize if both rounding directions are possible

    score = fractional_score * objective_score - lock_penalty - pscost_penalty - exploration_penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if obj >= 0:
        if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and candsfrac > 0.5):
            roundup = True
    else:
        if pscostup > pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and candsfrac <= 0.5):
            roundup = True

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac) * (1 / (nlocksup + 1)) * (2 if isBinary else 1)
    else:
        score = -obj * objnorm * candsfrac * (1 / (nlocksdown + 1)) * (2 if isBinary else 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction based on objective function coefficient sign and fractional part
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = (candsfrac > 0.5)
    else:
        roundup = (candsfrac <= 0.5)

    # Compute score based on objective function coefficient, pseudo-cost, and fractional part
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (pscostup + 1e-6)

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    penalty_down = 1.0 if mayrounddown else 10.0
    penalty_up = 1.0 if mayroundup else 10.0
    
    obj_sign = -1.0 if obj < 0 else 1.0
    
    roundup_score = obj_sign * obj * penalty_up + (0.5 - candsfrac) * objnorm / (pscostup + 1e-6) + (candsol - rootsolval) * obj_sign
    rounddown_score = obj_sign * obj * penalty_down + candsfrac * objnorm / (pscostdown + 1e-6) + (rootsolval - candsol) * obj_sign
    
    score = max(roundup_score, rounddown_score)
    roundup = (roundup_score > rounddown_score)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine the rounding direction
    if pscostdown < pscostup:
        roundup = False
        feasibility_penalty = 10 if not mayrounddown else 0
    else:
        roundup = True
        feasibility_penalty = 10 if not mayroundup else 0

    # Calculate the score
    score = abs(obj) / objnorm * (1 - candsfrac) - feasibility_penalty
    if isBinary:
        score -= 5  # Penalize binary variables
    score -= nNonz * 0.1  # Penalize variables with more non-zero coefficients

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if pscostdown < pscostup and mayrounddown:
        roundup = False
    elif pscostup < pscostdown and mayroundup:
        roundup = True
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    scoreup = 0.0
    scoredown = 0.0
    
    if mayroundup:
        scoreup = -obj * (1.0 - candsfrac) - pscostup
    else:
        scoreup = -1e10
        
    if mayrounddown:
        scoredown = obj * candsfrac - pscostdown
    else:
        scoredown = -1e10
        
    if isBinary:
        scoreup *= 1.5
        scoredown *= 1.5
        
    if nlocksdown > nlocksup:
        scoreup *= 1.2
    elif nlocksup > nlocksdown:
        scoredown *= 1.2
        
    score = max(scoreup, scoredown)
    roundup = (scoreup >= scoredown)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_des>
The new score function considers several factors to determine the rounding direction and score for each variable. The primary objective is to explore different directions and avoid getting stuck in a local optimum. Firstly, it penalizes variables that cannot be rounded in either direction to promote more exploration. Secondly, it favors rounding directions that move the variable's value closer to its root solution value, if available. Additionally, it incorporates objective function information, preferring to round variables with higher objective coefficients in the direction that minimizes the objective value. The score also takes into account the variable's type (binary or general integer), the number of non-zeros, and the pseudo-costs associated with rounding up or down.
</end_des>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    score = 0
    roundup = False

    # Penalize variables that cannot be rounded in either direction
    if not mayrounddown and not mayroundup:
        score -= 1000

    # Favor rounding towards the root solution value, if available
    if rootsolval != 0:
        roundup = candsol < rootsolval
    else:
        # If no root solution value, favor rounding towards integer based on fractional part
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    # Incorporate objective function information
    if obj < 0:
        roundup = not roundup
    score += abs(obj) / objnorm

    # Adjust score based on variable type and number of non-zeros
    if isBinary:
        score += 10
    score += nNonz / 10

    # Consider pseudo-costs for rounding up or down
    if roundup:
        score -= pscostup
    else:
        score -= pscostdown

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The new heuristic scoring function, called `myheurdiving`, aims to prioritize variables with larger objective coefficients and fractional values closer to 0.5, while also considering the potential impact on feasibility. <start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = mayroundup
    else:
        roundup = mayrounddown

    # Calculate score components
    abs_obj_score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    dist_from_half_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    feasibility_penalty = 0
    if (roundup and not mayroundup) or (not roundup and not mayrounddown):
        feasibility_penalty = 10

    # Calculate final score
    score = 0.6 * abs_obj_score + 0.3 * (1 - dist_from_half_score) - 0.1 * feasibility_penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

        penalty *= 0.1

        penalty *= 0.1

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    score = 0
    roundup = False

    # Penalize variables that cannot be rounded in either direction
    if not mayrounddown and not mayroundup:
        score -= 1000

    # Determine rounding direction based on objective function coefficient and fractional part
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = (candsfrac > 0.5)
    else:
        roundup = (candsfrac <= 0.5)

    # Compute score based on objective function coefficient, pseudo-cost, and fractional part
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (pscostup + 1e-6)
        else:
            score = -1e8  # Penalize infeasible rounding
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = -obj * candsfrac / (pscostdown + 1e-6)
        else:
            score = -1e8  # Penalize infeasible rounding

    # Adjust score based on other features
    score *= (1 + nNonz / 100)  # Prioritize variables with more non-zeros
    score *= (1 + isBinary)  # Slightly prioritize binary variables

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if pscostdown < pscostup:
        roundup = False
        potential_cost = pscostdown
    else:
        roundup = True
        potential_cost = pscostup

    # Calculate score
    score = obj / objnorm - potential_cost

    if (not mayrounddown and not roundup) or (not mayroundup and roundup):
        score -= 10000

    # Prioritize binary variables
    if isBinary:

    roundup = False

    # Penalize variables that cannot be rounded in either direction
    if not mayrounddown and not mayroundup:
        score -= 1000

    # Favor rounding towards the root solution value, if available
    if rootsolval != 0:
        roundup = candsol < rootsolval
    else:
        # If no root solution value, favor rounding towards integer based on fractional part
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    # Incorporate objective function information
    if obj < 0:
        roundup = not roundup
    score += abs(obj) / objnorm

    # Adjust score based on variable type and number of non-zeros
    if isBinary:
        score += 10
    score += nNonz / 10

    # Consider pseudo-costs for rounding up or down
    if roundup:
        score -= pscostup
    else:
        score -= pscostdown

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The new score function primarily aims to guide the rounding direction by considering the potential impact on the objective function and the feasibility of the rounding operation. It favors rounding in the direction that minimizes the objective function while prioritizing feasible rounding options. Additionally, it incorporates features like the pseudo cost and the root solution value to capture the problem's history and context.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    score = 0.0
    
    # Penalize infeasible rounding directions
    if not mayrounddown:
        score -= 1e6
    if not mayroundup:
        score -= 1e6
    
    # Consider objective function impact
    obj_impact_down = obj * (candsol - max(0, candsol - 1))
    obj_impact_up = obj * (min(1, candsol + 1) - candsol)
    
    # Prioritize rounding towards the root solution value
    root_diff_down = abs(max(0, candsol - 1) - rootsolval)
    root_diff_up = abs(min(1, candsol + 1) - rootsolval)
    
    # Combine objective function impact and root solution value difference
    score_down = obj_impact_down + root_diff_down
    score_up = obj_impact_up + root_diff_up
    
    # Consider pseudo costs
    if isBinary:
        score_down += pscostdown
        score_up += pscostup
    
    # Choose the better rounding direction
    if score_down < score_up:
        score = score_down
    else:
        score = score_up
        roundup = True
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The score is then calculated based on the fractional part of the variable's solution value, the number of non-zeros in the variable's column, and whether the variable is binary. Rounding directions that are not allowed are given a low score.

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    score_down = max(0, obj) * candsfrac - pscostdown - nlocksdown * objnorm / 100
    score_up = min(0, obj) * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostup - nlocksup * objnorm / 100
    
    if mayrounddown and mayroundup:
        if score_down > score_up:
            roundup = False
            score = score_down + (1 - candsfrac) * (1 + nNonz) * (1 if isBinary else 2)
        else:
            roundup = True
            score = score_up + candsfrac * (1 + nNonz) * (1 if isBinary else 2)
    elif mayrounddown:
        roundup = False
        score = score_down + (1 - candsfrac) * (1 + nNonz) * (1 if isBinary else 2)
    elif mayroundup:
        roundup = True
        score = score_up + candsfrac * (1 + nNonz) * (1 if isBinary else 2)
    else:
        roundup = False
        score = -1e9
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if obj >= 0:
        if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and candsfrac > 0.5):
            roundup = True
    else:
        if pscostup > pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and candsfrac <= 0.5):
            roundup = True

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac) * (1 / (nlocksup + 1)) * (1 - nNonz / (candsol + 1) if isBinary else 1)
    else:
        score = -obj * objnorm * candsfrac * (1 / (nlocksdown + 1)) * (1 - nNonz / (candsol + 1) if isBinary else 1)

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    score = 0
    roundup = False

    # Penalize variables that cannot be rounded in either direction
    if not mayrounddown and not mayroundup:
        score -= 1000

    # Favor rounding towards the root solution value, if available
    if rootsolval != 0:
        roundup = candsol > rootsolval
    else:
        # If no root solution value, favor rounding towards integer based on fractional part
        roundup = candsfrac < 0.5

    # Incorporate objective function information
    if obj > 0:
        roundup = not roundup
    score += abs(obj) / (objnorm + 1e-6)  # Avoid division by zero

    # Adjust score based on variable type and number of non-zeros
    if isBinary:
        score += 20
    score += nNonz / 5

    # Consider pseudo-costs for rounding up or down
    if roundup:
        score += pscostdown
    else:
        score += pscostup

    # Heuristic adjustments
    if nlocksdown > nlocksup:
        score += 5  # Favor rounding down if more locks for rounding down
    elif nlocksdown < nlocksup:
        score -= 5  # Favor rounding up if more locks for rounding up

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = False
    else:
        roundup = True

    # Calculate score
    score = abs(obj) / objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)

    # Penalize variables with locks
    if (not mayrounddown and roundup) or (not mayroundup and not roundup):
        score *= 0.1

    # Consider pseudo-cost information
    if roundup:
        score += pscostup
    else:
        score -= pscostdown

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = mayroundup
    else:
        roundup = mayrounddown

    # Calculate score components
    abs_obj_score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    dist_from_half_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    feasibility_penalty = 0
    if (roundup and not mayroundup) or (not roundup and not mayrounddown):
        feasibility_penalty = 10

    # Calculate final score
    score = 0.6 * abs_obj_score + 0.3 * (1 - dist_from_half_score) - 0.1 * feasibility_penalty

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = mayroundup
    else:
        roundup = mayrounddown

    # Calculate score components
    abs_obj_score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    dist_from_half_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    pseudo_cost_score = min(pscostdown, pscostup) / max(1e-6, max(pscostdown, pscostup))
    feasibility_penalty = 0
    if (roundup and not mayroundup) or (not roundup and not mayrounddown):
        feasibility_penalty = 10
    binary_bonus = 1.5 if isBinary else 1.0

    # Calculate final score
    score = 0.4 * abs_obj_score + 0.3 * (1 - dist_from_half_score) + 0.2 * pseudo_cost_score - 0.1 * feasibility_penalty
    score *= binary_bonus

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if obj > 0:
        roundup = pscostup <= pscostdown
    else:
        roundup = pscostup > pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    fractional_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    objective_score = abs(obj / objnorm) if objnorm > 0 else abs(obj)
    lock_penalty = (nlocksdown + nlocksup) / max(1, nNonz)
    exploration_penalty = 0.1 if (mayrounddown and mayroundup) else 1.0
    score = fractional_score * objective_score / (lock_penalty + exploration_penalty)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 1.5
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

The new heuristic scoring function aims to balance multiple factors, including objective coefficient, fractional part, pseudo-costs, potential feasibility violations, and variable type. It prioritizes variables with larger objective coefficients and fractional values closer to 0.5, while penalizing variables that cannot be rounded in either direction. Binary variables and variables with higher pseudo-costs are favored, and the rounding direction is chosen based on the objective coefficient sign and feasibility.
</end_des>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = mayroundup
    else:
        roundup = mayrounddown

    # Calculate score components
    abs_obj_score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    dist_from_half_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    pseudo_cost_score = min(pscostdown, pscostup) / max(1e-6, max(pscostdown, pscostup))
    feasibility_penalty = 0
    if (roundup and not mayroundup) or (not roundup and not mayrounddown):
        feasibility_penalty = 10
    binary_bonus = 1.5 if isBinary else 1.0

    # Calculate final score
    score = 0.4 * abs_obj_score + 0.3 * (1 - dist_from_half_score) + 0.2 * pseudo_cost_score - 0.1 * feasibility_penalty
    score *= binary_bonus

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if obj >= 0:
        roundup = mayroundup
    else:
        roundup = mayrounddown

    # Calculate score components
    abs_obj_score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    dist_from_half_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    pseudo_cost_score = min(pscostdown, pscostup) / max(1e-6, max(pscostdown, pscostup))
    feasibility_penalty = 0
    if (roundup and not mayroundup) or (not roundup and not mayrounddown):
        feasibility_penalty = 10
    binary_bonus = 1.5 if isBinary else 1.0
    pseudo_cost_bonus = 1 / (1 + min(pscostdown, pscostup))

    # Calculate final score
    score = 0.4 * abs_obj_score + 0.3 * (1 - dist_from_half_score) + 0.2 * pseudo_cost_score - 0.1 * feasibility_penalty
    score *= binary_bonus * pseudo_cost_bonus

    return score, roundup

The score function aims to balance the objective function contribution, fractional value, potential locking effects, and exploration aspects. <start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if obj > 0:
        roundup = pscostup <= pscostdown
    else:
        roundup = pscostup > pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    fractional_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    objective_score = abs(obj / objnorm) if objnorm > 0 else abs(obj)
    lock_penalty = (nlocksdown + nlocksup) / max(1, nNonz)
    exploration_penalty = 0.1 if (mayrounddown and mayroundup) else 1.0
    score = fractional_score * objective_score / (lock_penalty + exploration_penalty)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

score:7.3007421965574
<start_des>
The new score function aims to prioritize variables that contribute more to the objective function while considering feasibility constraints. It first determines the rounding direction based on the pseudo-cost and the objective function value. If rounding up has a lower pseudo-cost or a higher objective function value, it rounds up; otherwise, it rounds down. The score is then calculated as the product of the objective function value and a penalty term that discourages rounding variables that may lead to infeasibility. The penalty term is larger for variables closer to being integer, encouraging more exploration.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1 / (abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1)
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    score = obj * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj >= 0):
        roundup = True

    # Calculate score components
    abs_obj_score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    dist_from_half_score = abs(candsfrac - 0.5)
    feasibility_penalty = 0
    if (roundup and not mayroundup) or (not roundup and not mayrounddown):
        feasibility_penalty = 10
    pseudo_cost_penalty = min(pscostdown, pscostup) / (abs(pscostdown - pscostup) + 1e-6)

    # Calculate final score

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 2.0
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.2

    rootdist = abs(candsol - rootsolval) / objnorm
    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) - rootdist

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if isBinary:
        roundup = pscostdown > pscostup
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    # Calculate score
    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (1 + candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (1 - candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    return score, roundup

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if obj > 0:
        if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and candsfrac > 0.5):
            roundup = True
    else:
        if pscostdown < pscostup or (pscostdown == pscostup and candsfrac < 0.5):
            roundup = True

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if not mayrounddown and not mayroundup:
        score -= 10000
    elif roundup and not mayroundup:
        score -= 1000
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        score -= 1000

    if abs(candsol - rootsolval) < 0.5:
        score += 1000

    score += nNonz

    if isBinary:
        score += 100

    return score, roundup

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 1.5
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup)

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction

        roundup = pscostdown > pscostup
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    # Calculate score

    if roundup:

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 2.0
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.2

    penalty *= 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)

    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) + nNonz * 0.05

The score function, named `myheurdiving`, aims to prioritize variables with fractional values closer to integers, while considering the objective function value, pseudo costs, and other factors. It first determines the rounding direction by comparing the potential impact on the objective function, taking into account the objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, and whether the variable is binary. The function favors variables with larger objective coefficients, smaller pseudo costs, and fewer locks.

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj >= 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.3
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.3

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 1.5
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

    penalty *= 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup + 0.1)

    abs_obj_score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    pseudo_cost_penalty = min(pscostdown, pscostup) / (abs(pscostdown - pscostup) + 1e-6)

    score = 0.5 * abs_obj_score + 0.3 * (1 - abs(candsfrac - 0.5)) - 0.1 * penalty - 0.1 * pseudo_cost_penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

It determines the rounding direction based on the pseudo-costs and objective function sign, and favors variables with more fractional parts for exploration. </end_des>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    exploration = abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1
    if isBinary:
        exploration *= 2.0
    else:
        exploration *= 1.5

    exploration *= 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)

    score = obj * penalty * exploration + nNonz * 0.05

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = pscostdown > pscostup
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.2
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.2

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (1 + candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (1 - candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup)

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.2
    else:
        score *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.2

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    if isBinary:
        roundup = (candsol > rootsolval)
        score = abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    else:
        if mayrounddown and mayroundup:
            roundup = (pscostup < pscostdown)
        elif mayrounddown:
            roundup = False
        else:
            roundup = True
        score = abs(obj / objnorm) - min(pscostdown, pscostup)
        score += (nlocksdown + nlocksup) * 0.1
    return score, roundup

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    exploration = abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

    if isBinary:
        exploration *= 1.5
    else:
        exploration *= 1.0

    score = abs(obj / objnorm) * exploration * penalty + nNonz * 0.05

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0)

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 1.5
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

    score = (obj / objnorm) * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) / (nNonz + max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

It considers the objective coefficient, fractional part, pseudo-costs, and the number of locks for each variable. <start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    if isBinary:
        roundup = pscostdown > pscostup
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    # Calculate score
    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (1 + candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (1 - candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    if isBinary:
        roundup = (candsol > rootsolval)
        score = abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    else:
        if mayrounddown and mayroundup:
            roundup = (pscostup < pscostdown)
        elif mayrounddown:
            roundup = False
        else:
            roundup = True
        score = abs(obj / objnorm) - min(pscostdown, pscostup)
        score += (nlocksdown + nlocksup) * 0.1
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

        penalty *= 0.2

        penalty *= 2.0

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 2.0
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.3

    rootdist = abs(candsol - rootsolval) / objnorm
    lockpenalty = 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown) if roundup else 1.0 / (1 + nlocksup)
    score = obj * penalty * lockpenalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) - rootdist + nNonz * 0.2

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = pscostdown > pscostup
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.5
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.5

    if roundup:
        score *= (0.8 + 0.2 * (1 - candsfrac)) / (1 + nlocksup)

        score *= 1.2

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup

</end_des>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.1
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.1

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 2.0
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.3

    rootdist = abs(candsol - rootsolval) / objnorm
    lockpenalty = 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown) if roundup else 1.0 / (1 + nlocksup)
    score = obj * penalty * lockpenalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) - rootdist + nNonz * 0.2

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.5
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.5

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 1.5
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.3

    penalty *= 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)

<start_des>
This scoring function aims to prioritize variables that are close to their integer values, have a larger impact on the objective function, and can be rounded in either direction without creating too many locks. Additionally, it favors rounding directions that lead to a smaller objective value, unless the variable's pseudo-cost suggests otherwise.
</end_des>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Compute fractional part distance from integer
    frac_dist = min(candsfrac, 1 - candsfrac)
    
    # Compute objective function impact
    obj_impact = abs(obj) / objnorm
    
    # Compute lock penalties
    lock_penalty_down = nlocksdown if mayrounddown else float('inf')
    lock_penalty_up = nlocksup if mayroundup else float('inf')
    
    # Compute pseudo-cost adjustments
    pseudo_adj_down = pscostdown if mayrounddown and pscostdown < 0 else 0
    pseudo_adj_up = pscostup if mayroundup and pscostup < 0 else 0
    
    # Compute rounding direction and score
    if obj < 0:
        roundup = True
        score = frac_dist * obj_impact + pseudo_adj_up - lock_penalty_up
    else:
        roundup = False
        score = frac_dist * obj_impact - pseudo_adj_down - lock_penalty_down
    
    # Adjust score for binary variables
    if isBinary:
        score *= 10
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.2

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 1.8
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.15

    penalty *= 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)

    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) + nNonz * 0.03 + objnorm * 0.1

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = pscostdown > pscostup
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.5
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.5

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (0.8 + 0.2 * (1 - candsfrac)) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (0.8 + 0.2 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - 0.5 * min(pscostdown, pscostup)

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.2
    else:
        score *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.3

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 100)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

        score *= (0.8 + 0.2 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) * 0.5

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.1
    else:
        score *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 100)

    return score, roundup

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.3
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.3

    if isBinary:
        penalty *= 1.8
    else:
        penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.4

    rootdist = abs(candsol - rootsolval) / objnorm
    penalty *= 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)

    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) - rootdist + nNonz * 0.15

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False

    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.25
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.25

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (0.85 - 0.15 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (0.85 + 0.15 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

The score function, named `myheurdiving`, aims to prioritize variables with fractional values closer to integers, while considering the objective function value, pseudo costs, and other factors. It first determines the rounding direction by comparing the potential impact on the objective function, taking into account the objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, and whether the variable is binary. The score is then calculated based on the distance from the fractional value to the nearest integer, weighted by the objective function coefficient and adjusted by the number of nonzero entries and locks. The function favors variables with larger objective coefficients, smaller pseudo costs, and fewer locks.

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
        else:
            score = -obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
        else:
            score = -obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The new score function aims to balance exploitation and exploration by considering objective contribution, pseudo-costs, fractional part, locks, infeasibility risks, and potential for improvement. It encourages exploring fractional variables with higher objective contributions but penalizes those with many locks or potential infeasibility. The score is calculated based on the objective function value, fractional part, number of nonzero entries, locks, pseudo-costs, and distance from the root solution. <start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.5  # Penalize potential infeasibility
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.5

    # Encourage exploration of fractional variables
    penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.2  
    penalty *= 2.0 if isBinary else 1.0  # Favor binary variables

    # Penalize variables with many locks
    lockpenalty = 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)
    penalty *= lockpenalty

    # Consider distance from root solution
    rootdist = abs(candsol - rootsolval) / objnorm
    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) - rootdist + nNonz * 0.2  # Favor variables with more nonzero entries

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    penalty *= 2.0 if isBinary else 1.0

    lockpenalty = 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)
    penalty *= lockpenalty

    rootdist = abs(candsol - rootsolval) / objnorm
    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) - rootdist + nNonz * 0.1

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = (pscostdown > pscostup) or (pscostdown == pscostup and obj > 0)
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.25
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.25

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (0.85 - 0.15 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (0.85 + 0.15 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) * 0.6

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.15
    else:

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 150)

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = (pscostdown >= pscostup) or (pscostdown == pscostup and obj > 0)
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac >= 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.6
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.6

    if roundup:

        score *= (0.9 + 0.2 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) * 0.7

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.1
    else:
        score *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 175)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The score function prioritizes variables with fractional values closer to integers, taking into account the objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, number of locks, and binary status. It determines the rounding direction based on the potential impact on the objective function and favors variables with larger objective coefficients, smaller pseudo costs, and fewer locks. The score is calculated based on a weighted combination of the distance from the fractional value to the nearest integer, objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, and the number of locks. Variables that can be rounded in the desired direction receive a higher score, while those that cannot be rounded are penalized.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score_up = obj * candsfrac - pscostup
    score_down = obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostdown
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = score_up / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
        else:
            score = -score_up / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = score_down / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
        else:
            score = -score_down / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if pscostup < pscostdown or (pscostup == pscostdown and obj > 0):
        roundup = True

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.5
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.5

    penalty *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.2
    penalty *= 1.5 if isBinary else 1.0

    lockpenalty = 1.0 / (1 + nlocksdown + nlocksup)
    penalty *= lockpenalty

    rootdist = abs(candsol - rootsolval) / objnorm
    score = obj * penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) - rootdist + nNonz * 0.2

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = (pscostdown > pscostup) or (pscostdown == pscostup and obj > 0)
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.25
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.25

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (0.85 - 0.15 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (0.85 + 0.15 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) * 0.6

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.15
    else:
        score *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.15

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 150)

    return score, roundup

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 150)

    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
        else:
            score = -obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
        else:
            score = -obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = (pscostdown > pscostup) or (pscostdown == pscostup and obj > 0)
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5 and abs(candsol - rootsolval) < abs(candsol - math.ceil(candsol) - rootsolval)

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.2
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.2

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (0.8 - 0.2 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (0.8 + 0.2 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup)

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.1
    else:
        score *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.1

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 150)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score_up = obj * candsfrac - pscostup
    score_down = obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostdown
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = score_up / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
        else:
            score = -score_up / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = score_down / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
        else:
            score = -score_down / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The rounding direction is determined by the objective sign, fractional part, and the ability to round without causing infeasibility. The score is higher for variables that can be rounded in the desired direction without causing infeasibility and lower for variables with many locks or high pseudo-costs.

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = (pscostdown > pscostup) or (pscostdown == pscostup and obj > 0)
    else:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5

    penalty = 1.0
    if roundup and not mayroundup:
        penalty *= 0.25
    elif not roundup and not mayrounddown:
        penalty *= 0.25

    score = abs(obj) / objnorm
    if roundup:
        score *= (0.9 - 0.1 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksup)
    else:
        score *= (0.9 + 0.1 * candsfrac) / (1 + nlocksdown)

    score *= penalty - min(pscostdown, pscostup) * 0.7

    if isBinary:
        score *= 1.1
    else:
        score *= abs(candsfrac - 0.5) + 0.2

    score *= (1 + nNonz / 200)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) * (1 - abs(candsol - rootsolval)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
        else:
            score = -obj * (1 - candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = obj * candsfrac * (1 - abs(candsol - rootsolval)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -obj * candsfrac / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score_up = obj * candsfrac - pscostup
    score_down = obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostdown
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = score_up / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
        else:
            score = -score_up / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = score_down / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
        else:
            score = -score_down / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (candsfrac - 0.5) > 0

    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = abs(candsfrac - 1) * obj / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
        else:
            score = -abs(candsfrac - 1) * obj / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = abs(candsfrac) * obj / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -abs(candsfrac) * obj / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + nlocksup + 1) * (pscostup + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + nlocksup + 1) * (pscostup + 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + nlocksdown + 1) * (pscostdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + nlocksdown + 1) * (pscostdown + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_des>
The score function considers the objective function coefficient, fractional part, pseudo costs, number of locks, binary status, and deviation from the root solution. It aims to prioritize variables with larger objective coefficients, fractional values closer to integers, and fewer locks. The score is calculated as the product of the objective coefficient, fractional part, and a penalty term that incorporates the number of locks, binary status, and deviation from the root solution. The penalty term penalizes variables with locks or larger deviations from the root solution, favoring those that can be rounded without locks or closer to the root solution. The rounding direction is determined based on the potential impact on the objective function, considering pseudo costs and favoring the direction with a larger objective coefficient contribution.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate penalty term
    penalty_up = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    penalty_down = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    if isBinary:
        penalty_up *= 0.5
        penalty_down *= 0.5

    # Calculate score
    score_up = obj * (1 - candsfrac) * objnorm * penalty_up
    score_down = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty_down
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = score_up
        else:
            score = -score_up
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = score_down
        else:
            score = -score_down

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score_up = (obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostup) / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    score_down = (obj * candsfrac - pscostdown) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = score_up * abs(1 - candsfrac)
        else:
            score = -score_up * abs(1 - candsfrac)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = score_down * abs(candsfrac)
        else:
            score = -score_down * abs(candsfrac)

    return score, roundup

It calculates the score as the product of the objective coefficient, the fractional part, the normalized objective coefficient, and a penalty term that discourages rounding in directions with locks or binary constraints. The penalty term incorporates the number of locks, the deviation from the minimum of root solution and 0.5 (for binary variables), and a factor based on whether rounding up or down is possible. The rounding direction is determined based on the potential impact on the objective function, factoring in pseudo costs and favoring variables with larger objective coefficients and fewer locks.

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + nlocksup + 1) * (pscostup + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + nlocksup + 1) * (pscostup + 1))

        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + nlocksdown + 1) * (pscostdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + nlocksdown + 1) * (pscostdown + 1))

    return score, roundup

The score function prioritizes variables with fractional values closer to integers, taking into account the objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, number of locks, and binary status. It determines the rounding direction based on the potential impact on the objective function and favors variables with larger objective coefficients, smaller pseudo costs, fewer locks, and a larger difference between the fractional value and the nearest integer. The score is calculated based on a weighted combination of the absolute difference between the fractional value and the nearest integer, objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, and the number of locks. Variables that can be rounded in the desired direction receive a higher score, while those that cannot be rounded or have a smaller impact on the objective function are penalized.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score_up = (obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostup) / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    score_down = (obj * candsfrac - pscostdown) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = score_up * abs(1 - candsfrac)
        else:
            score = -score_up * abs(1 - candsfrac)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = score_down * abs(candsfrac)
        else:
            score = -score_down * abs(candsfrac)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

It calculates the score as the product of the objective coefficient, the fractional part, the normalized objective coefficient, and a penalty term that discourages rounding in directions with locks or binary constraints. The penalty term incorporates the number of locks, the deviation from the minimum of root solution and 0.5 (for binary variables), and a factor based on whether rounding up or down is possible. <start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = (1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) + (pscostup / objnorm)

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The score function prioritizes variables with fractional values closer to integers, considering the objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, number of locks, binary status, and a combination of the minimum distance from the root solution and 0.5 (for binary variables). It calculates the score as the product of the objective coefficient, the fractional part, the normalized objective coefficient, and a penalty term that incorporates the number of locks and the deviation from the minimum of root solution and 0.5 (for binary variables), with a dampening factor based on the number of non-zeros. The rounding direction is determined based on the potential impact on the objective function, factoring in pseudo costs and favoring variables with larger objective coefficients and fewer locks.

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5))))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5))))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5))))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5))))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = (1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) + (pscostup / objnorm)
        else:
            penalty = -(1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) - (pscostup / objnorm)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = (1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) + (pscostdown / objnorm)
        else:
            penalty = -(1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) - (pscostdown / objnorm)

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>
