def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))

    score = (obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty) / ((nNonz + 1) * (min(pscostdown, pscostup) + 1))

    return score, roundup

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

It determines the rounding direction based on the potential impact on the objective function, favoring variables with larger objective coefficients and smaller pseudo costs. The score is calculated based on the distance from the fractional value to the nearest integer, weighted by the objective function coefficient, and penalized for variables that cannot be rounded in the desired direction or have many locks. Additionally, it incorporates a penalty for variables with more non-zero entries, encouraging the selection of sparser variables.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The score function prioritizes variables with fractional values closer to integers, considering the objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, number of locks, binary status, and the minimum distance from the root solution and 0.5 (for binary variables). It calculates the score as the product of the objective coefficient, the fractional part, the normalized objective coefficient, and a penalty term that discourages rounding in directions with locks or binary constraints. The penalty term incorporates the number of locks, the deviation from the minimum of root solution and 0.5 (for binary variables), and a factor based on whether rounding up or down is possible. The rounding direction is determined based on the potential impact on the objective function, factoring in pseudo costs and favoring variables with larger objective coefficients and fewer locks.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = (1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) + (pscostup / objnorm)
        else:
            penalty = -(1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) - (pscostup / objnorm)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = (1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) + (pscostdown / objnorm)
        else:
            penalty = -(1 / (nNonz + 1)) * (1 / (nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))) - (pscostdown / objnorm)

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))

    score = (obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty) / ((nNonz + 1) * (min(pscostdown, pscostup) + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    if roundup:

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostup))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostup))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostdown))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / ((nNonz + 1) ** 0.5 * (nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostdown))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsfrac > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:

The score function considers the objective function coefficient, fractional part of the variable, deviation from the root solution, pseudo costs, number of locks, binary status, and sparsity. It prioritizes variables with larger objective coefficients, fractional values closer to integers, and smaller deviations from the root solution. The rounding direction is determined by the potential improvement in the objective function, accounting for pseudo costs, objective coefficients, and binary constraints. <start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + min(abs(candsol - rootsolval), abs(candsol - 0.5)))

    score = (obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty) / ((nNonz + 1) * (min(pscostdown, pscostup) + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev ** 2)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev ** 2)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev ** 2)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev ** 2)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:

        else:

    return score, roundup

    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:

        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / ((nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostup) ** 0.7)
        else:
            penalty = -1 / ((nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostup) ** 0.7)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / ((nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostdown) ** 0.7)
        else:
            penalty = -1 / ((nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + pscostdown) ** 0.7)

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup

    # Determine rounding direction

    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score = 0

        if mayroundup:

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = 1 / (nlocksdown + nlocksup + 1)
    penalty_sparsity = 1 / (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 / (abs(candsol - rootsolval) + 1)

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac * objnorm - pscostup) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac * objnorm - pscostup) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac) * objnorm - pscostdown) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac) * objnorm - pscostdown) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_pscost = pscostup if roundup else pscostdown
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    penalty_nonzeros = nNonz + 1

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

It calculates the score as the product of the objective coefficient, the fractional part, the normalized objective coefficient, and a penalty term that discourages rounding in directions with locks or binary constraints. The penalty term incorporates the number of locks, the deviation from the root solution, and a factor based on whether rounding up or down is possible. The rounding direction is determined based on the potential impact on the objective function, factoring in pseudo costs and favoring variables with larger objective coefficients and fewer locks.
</end_des>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksdown + 1))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The score function combines multiple factors to prioritize variables with larger objective coefficients, fractional values closer to integers, and rounding directions that potentially improve the objective value. It incorporates penalties for locks, sparsity, and deviations from root solutions to encourage exploration and diversification. The score is calculated as a weighted combination of the objective coefficient, fractional part, pseudo costs, and penalties based on locks, sparsity, and distance from the root solution. The rounding direction is determined by comparing the potential objective value improvement for rounding up or down, considering pseudo costs and favoring variables with larger objective coefficients and fewer locks.

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = 1 / (nlocksdown + nlocksup + 1)
    penalty_sparsity = 1 / (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 / (abs(candsol - rootsolval) + 1)

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac * objnorm - pscostup) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac * objnorm - pscostup) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac) * objnorm - pscostdown) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac) * objnorm - pscostdown) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = pscostup < pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    penalty_binary = 1 if isBinary else 0

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac - pscostup) / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + abs(candsol - 1))
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac - pscostup) / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + abs(candsol - 1))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostdown) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + abs(candsol))
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac) - pscostdown) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) + abs(candsol))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_pscost = pscostup if roundup else pscostdown
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    penalty_nonzeros = nNonz + 1

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * (1 / candsfrac) * objnorm) / (pscostup * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 / candsfrac) * objnorm) / (pscostup * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 / (1 - candsfrac)) * objnorm) / (pscostdown * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 / (1 - candsfrac)) * objnorm) / (pscostdown * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = 1 / (nlocksdown + nlocksup + 1)
    penalty_sparsity = 1 / (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 / (abs(candsol - rootsolval) + 1)

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * candsfrac * objnorm - pscostup) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
        else:
            score = -(obj * candsfrac * objnorm - pscostup) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 - candsfrac) * objnorm - pscostdown) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 - candsfrac) * objnorm - pscostdown) * penalty_locks * penalty_sparsity * penalty_rootdev

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = pscostup < pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    penalty_binary = 1 if isBinary else 0

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * (1 + penalty_binary))

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

    # Calculate score
    score = 0

    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_pscost = pscostup if roundup else pscostdown
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    penalty_nonzeros = nNonz + 1

            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)
        else:

        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac)) / (penalty_pscost * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev * penalty_nonzeros)

    return score, roundup

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
        else:
            score = -obj * candsfrac / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)
        else:
            score = -obj * (1 - candsfrac) / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

The score function prioritizes variables with fractional values closer to integers, considering the objective function coefficient, pseudo costs, number of locks, binary status, and the distance from the root solution. It calculates the score as the product of the objective coefficient, the fractional part, the normalized objective coefficient, and a penalty term that discourages rounding in directions with locks or binary constraints. The penalty term incorporates the number of locks, the deviation from the root solution, and a factor based on whether rounding up or down is possible. The rounding direction is determined based on the potential impact on the objective function, factoring in pseudo costs and favoring variables with larger objective coefficients and fewer locks.

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty = 1
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksup + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            penalty = 1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))
        else:
            penalty = -1 / (nNonz + nlocksdown + 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval))

    score = obj * candsfrac * objnorm * penalty

    return score, roundup
</end_code>

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * (1 / candsfrac) * objnorm) / (pscostup * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 / candsfrac) * objnorm) / (pscostup * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * (1 / (1 - candsfrac)) * objnorm) / (pscostdown * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * (1 / (1 - candsfrac)) * objnorm) / (pscostdown * penalty_locks * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

import math

def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = obj * candsfrac * (1 - abs(candsol - rootsolval)) / ((nNonz + 1) * (nlocksup + 1))

        else:

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = obj * (1 - candsfrac) < obj * candsfrac - pscostup + pscostdown
    
    # Calculate score
    score = 0
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1)
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval)
    
    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (2 * candsfrac - candsfrac**2)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (2 * candsfrac - candsfrac**2)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (2 * (1 - candsfrac) - (1 - candsfrac)**2)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (2 * (1 - candsfrac) - (1 - candsfrac)**2)) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev)
    
    return score, roundup
</end_code>

<start_code>
def myheurdiving(mayrounddown, mayroundup, candsfrac, candsol, nlocksdown, nlocksup, obj, objnorm, pscostdown, pscostup, rootsolval, nNonz, isBinary):
    # Determine rounding direction
    roundup = False
    if isBinary:
        roundup = candsol > 0.5
    else:
        roundup = pscostup < pscostdown

    # Calculate score
    penalty_locks = max(nlocksdown, nlocksup) + 1
    penalty_nonzeros = (nNonz + 1) ** 2
    penalty_rootdev = 1 + abs(candsol - rootsolval) ** 2
    penalty_binary = 2 if isBinary else 1

    if roundup:
        if mayroundup:
            score = (obj * objnorm * candsfrac ** 2) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * penalty_binary)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * candsfrac ** 2) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * penalty_binary)
    else:
        if mayrounddown:
            score = (obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac) ** 2) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * penalty_binary)
        else:
            score = -(obj * objnorm * (1 - candsfrac) ** 2) / (penalty_locks * penalty_nonzeros * penalty_rootdev * penalty_binary)

    return score, roundup
</end_code>
